namespace = underdeep

# Dungeon Delving events.
# The text for every event is in localization/english/underdeep_events_l_english.yml, under the same id.
# 1001 entrance, 1101-1199 rooms, 1201-1269 encounters, 5001-5099 company events, 6001-6002 bosses
# (6101 win, 6201 loss, 6301 company fight, 6401-6403 the single combat), 7001-7005 endings, 8001-8105 Gandalf and Saruman, 1255 and 1260 special,
# 9001-9003 the murder, woo and befriend intents, 1401-1403 the junctions (doors, tunnels, the pit).
# The hidden events that run the delve (start, what comes next, end) are in underdeep_engine.txt.

# 1401: The Way Divides (doors)
underdeep.1401 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.1401.t
	desc = underdeep.1401.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = dungeon }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = worry }
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		underdeep_setup_junction_effect = yes
		underdeep_pick_rooms_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_treasure_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_treasure } stewardship >= 12 }
		skill = stewardship
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_read_stewardship
		underdeep_enter_room_effect = { KIND = treasure }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_treasure_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_treasure } stewardship < 12 intrigue >= 12 }
		skill = intrigue
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_read_intrigue
		underdeep_enter_room_effect = { KIND = treasure }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_treasure_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_treasure } stewardship < 12 intrigue < 12 has_activity_intent = underdeep_lore_intent learning >= 8 }
		skill = learning
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_read_lore
		underdeep_enter_room_effect = { KIND = treasure }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_treasure_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_treasure } stewardship < 12 intrigue < 12 NOT = { has_activity_intent = underdeep_lore_intent learning >= 8 } }
		underdeep_enter_room_effect = { KIND = treasure }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_danger_read_name

		skill = prowess

		skill = martial
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_read_martial
		underdeep_enter_room_effect = { KIND = danger }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_danger_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_danger } prowess < 12 martial < 12 has_activity_intent = underdeep_lore_intent learning >= 8 }

	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_whispers_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_whispers } learning >= 12 }
		skill = learning
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_read_learning
		underdeep_enter_room_effect = { KIND = whispers }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_whispers_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_whispers } learning < 12 has_activity_intent = underdeep_lore_intent learning >= 8 }
		skill = learning

	}

		name = underdeep_door_whispers_name

		skill = diplomacy
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_read_diplomacy

		name = underdeep_door_rest_read_name
		trigger = { involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_show_rest } diplomacy < 12 has_activity_intent = underdeep_lore_intent learning >= 8 }

		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_door_conqueror
		trigger = { underdeep_conqueror_door_shown = yes has_trait = conqueror }
		show_as_unavailable = { underdeep_conqueror_door_shown = yes NOT = { has_trait = conqueror } }
		trait = conqueror
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_door_conqueror_tt
		hidden_effect = { trigger_event = { id = underdeep.1111 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_leave_option
		flavor = underdeep_leave_option_flavor
		underdeep_end_with = { ENDING = 7001 }
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# 1403: The Drop (pit)
underdeep.1403 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.1403.t
	desc = underdeep.1403.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = dungeon }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = worry }
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = underdeep.1403.a
		flavor = underdeep.1403.a.flavor
		random = {
			chance = 40
			underdeep_wound_effect = yes
		}
		underdeep_pit_room_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1403.b
		flavor = underdeep.1403.b.flavor
		trigger = { prowess < 25 }
		skill = prowess
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = underdeep_check_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1403.b.success
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1403.b.failure
				underdeep_wound_effect = yes
				add_prestige = -50
			}
		}
		underdeep_pit_room_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1403.d
		flavor = underdeep.1403.d.flavor
		trigger = { prowess >= 25 }
		add_prestige = 100
		underdeep_glory_opinion_effect = yes
		underdeep_pit_room_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_leave_option
		flavor = underdeep_leave_option_flavor
		underdeep_end_with = { ENDING = 7001 }
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# 1001: Under the Stone
underdeep.1001 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.1001.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:8105 this = character:tfw_lineofnixni_001 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_8105_nixni
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:8075 this = character:tfw_lineofnixni_001 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_8075_nixni
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:9191 this = character:tfw_lineofnixni_001 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_9191_nixni
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:5038 this = character:tfw_lineofnixni_001 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_5038_nixni
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:8105 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_8105
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:8075 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_8075
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:9191 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_9191
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:5038 }
				desc = underdeep.1001.open_5038
			}
		}
		desc = underdeep.1001.desc
	}
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = fp3_cave }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = worry }
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_1
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_1 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = fear
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_scene_cast_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1001.a
		flavor = underdeep.1001.a.flavor
		if = { limit = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:8105 } underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = start_8105 } }
		else_if = { limit = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:8075 } underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = start_8075 } }
		else_if = { limit = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:9191 } underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = start_9191 } }
		else_if = { limit = { involved_activity.activity_location = province:5038 } underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = start_5038 } }
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1001.b
		flavor = underdeep.1001.b.flavor
		underdeep_turn_back_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

# 1101: The Frozen Stair
underdeep.1101 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.1101.t
	desc = underdeep.1101.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = fp3_cave }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 1101 } } }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = paranoia }
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_1
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_1 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3

	}

	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }

			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 1101 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 1101 }
		underdeep_scene_cast_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1101.a
		flavor = underdeep.1101.a.flavor

			type = event_toast_effect_neutral

	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1101.b
		flavor = underdeep.1101.b.flavor

			type = event_toast_effect_neutral

		}

			skill = learning
			value = underdeep_check_hard

				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1101.c.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.1101.c.success
					left_icon = root
					underdeep_delver_xp_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
					underdeep_trait_find_effect = { TRAIT = lifestyle_mystic VALUE = 90 }
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1101_s }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1101.c.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad

					left_icon = root

		}

# 5001: Words at the Halt
underdeep.5001 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.5001.t
	desc = underdeep.5001.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = ce1_catacombs }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 5001 } } }
	left_portrait = { character = scope:delver animation = anger }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:delver_two animation = anger }
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = worry
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = worry
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = worry
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = wrathful has_trait = arrogant } } }
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { count >= 2 NOT = { this = root } is_adult = yes location = root.location } }
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 5001 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 5001 }
		involved_activity = { random_attending_character = { limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = wrathful has_trait = arrogant } } alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } } weight = { base = 1 modifier = { add = 20 has_activity_intent = underdeep_prove_intent } } save_scope_as = delver } }
		involved_activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } }
				alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } }
				save_scope_as = delver_two
			}
		}
		underdeep_scene_cast_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5001.a
		flavor = underdeep.5001.a.flavor
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = underdeep.5001.a
			left_icon = root
			add_stress = 15
			random = { chance = 60 underdeep_party_duel_effect = yes }
			underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5001_d }
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5001.b
		flavor = underdeep.5001.b.flavor
		skill = diplomacy
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = underdeep_check_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.5001.b.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.5001.b.success
					left_icon = root
					underdeep_bond_effect = { A = scope:delver B = scope:delver_two MAJOR = no }
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5001_d }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.5001.b.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = underdeep.5001.b.failure
					left_icon = root
					add_stress = 15
					underdeep_party_duel_effect = yes
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5001_d }
				}
			}
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5001.c
		flavor = underdeep.5001.c.flavor
		skill = martial
		duel = {
			skill = martial
			value = underdeep_check_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.5001.c.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.5001.c.success
					left_icon = root
					add_dread = 10
					scope:delver = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_bad } }
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5001_d }

			}
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5001.d
		flavor = underdeep.5001.d.flavor
		trigger = {
			has_trait = just
		}
		trait = just
		custom_tooltip = underdeep_tt_why_just
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral

		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5001.e
		flavor = underdeep.5001.e.flavor
		trigger = {
			OR = { has_trait = sadistic has_trait = callous }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_trait = sadistic } custom_tooltip = underdeep_tt_why_sadistic }
		else_if = { limit = { has_trait = callous } custom_tooltip = underdeep_tt_why_callous }

# 5007: The Cold Fire
underdeep.5007 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.5007.t
	desc = underdeep.5007.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = ce1_catacombs }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 5007 } } }
	left_portrait = { character = scope:delver animation = fear }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:delver_two animation = worry }
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2

		animation = worry
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = worry
	}

		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = worry
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = craven } } }
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { count >= 2 NOT = { this = root } is_adult = yes location = root.location } }
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 5007 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 5007 }
		involved_activity = { random_attending_character = { limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = craven } } alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } } weight = { base = 1 modifier = { add = 20 has_activity_intent = underdeep_prove_intent } } save_scope_as = delver } }
		involved_activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } OR = { has_trait = brave } }

				save_scope_as = delver_two

			value = underdeep_check_value

				desc = underdeep.5007.b.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.5007.b.success
					left_icon = root

# 5011: A Debt to the Dead
underdeep.5011 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.5011.t
	desc = underdeep.5011.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = ce1_catacombs }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 5011 } } }
	left_portrait = { character = scope:delver animation = personality_honorable }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:delver_two animation = personality_cynical }
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = worry
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = worry
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = worry
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = zealous has_trait = honest has_trait = stubborn } } }
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { count >= 2 NOT = { this = root } is_adult = yes location = root.location } }
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 5011 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 5011 }
		involved_activity = { random_attending_character = { limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = zealous has_trait = honest has_trait = stubborn } } alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } } weight = { base = 1 modifier = { add = 20 has_activity_intent = underdeep_prove_intent } } save_scope_as = delver } }
		involved_activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } OR = { has_trait = cynical has_trait = greedy } }
				alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } }
				save_scope_as = delver_two
			}
		}
		underdeep_scene_cast_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5011.a
		flavor = underdeep.5011.a.flavor
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = underdeep.5011.a
			left_icon = root
			scope:delver = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_good } }
			underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5011_d }
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5011.b
		flavor = underdeep.5011.b.flavor
		skill = diplomacy
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = underdeep_check_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.5011.b.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.5011.b.success
					left_icon = root
					add_prestige = 100
					underdeep_bond_effect = { A = scope:delver B = scope:delver_two MAJOR = no }
					scope:delver = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_good } }
					scope:delver_two = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_good } }
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5011_d }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.5011.b.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = underdeep.5011.b.failure
					left_icon = root
					add_stress = 15
					scope:delver = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_bad } }
					scope:delver_two = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_bad } }
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5011_d }
				}
			}
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5011.c
		flavor = underdeep.5011.c.flavor

# 5014: Fetching Water
underdeep.5014 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.5014.t
	desc = underdeep.5014.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = ce1_catacombs }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 5014 } } }
	left_portrait = { character = scope:delver animation = personality_bold }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:delver_two animation = sadness }
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = worry
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = worry
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = worry
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = brave has_trait = compassionate has_trait = loyal } } }
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { count >= 2 NOT = { this = root } is_adult = yes location = root.location } }
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 5014 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 5014 }
		involved_activity = { random_attending_character = { limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } OR = { has_trait = brave has_trait = compassionate has_trait = loyal } } alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } } weight = { base = 1 modifier = { add = 20 has_activity_intent = underdeep_prove_intent } } save_scope_as = delver } }
		involved_activity = {
			random_attending_character = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes location = root.location NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } OR = { has_trait = delver } }
				alternative_limit = { NOT = { this = root } NOT = { this = scope:delver } }
				save_scope_as = delver_two
			}
		}
		underdeep_scene_cast_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5014.a
		flavor = underdeep.5014.a.flavor
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = underdeep.5014.a
			left_icon = root
			add_stress = 30
			scope:delver = { underdeep_party_death_effect = yes }
			underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5014_d }
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.5014.b
		flavor = underdeep.5014.b.flavor
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = underdeep.5014.b
			left_icon = root
			underdeep_supplies_effect = { AMOUNT = -250 GOLD = 50 }
			underdeep_bond_effect = { A = scope:delver B = scope:delver_two MAJOR = no }
			underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 5014_d }
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {

			50 = {

		}

	}

		save_scope_value_as = { name = ud_boss_prowess value = underdeep_duel_value_6001 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { scope:ud_boss ?= { is_alive = yes } } } underdeep_boss_duelist_6001_effect = yes scope:ud_boss = { add_prowess_skill = scope:ud_boss_prowess } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_fight_opt
		skill = prowess
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = underdeep_fight_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep_fight_win
				hidden_effect = { trigger_event = { id = underdeep.6101 days = 1 } }
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep_fight_lose
				hidden_effect = { trigger_event = { id = underdeep.6201 days = 1 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.6002.t
	desc = {
		desc = underdeep.6002.fight
		desc = underdeep_fight_odds_base
		triggered_desc = { trigger = { underdeep_fight_ease_delver > 0 } desc = underdeep_fight_odds_delver }
		triggered_desc = { trigger = { underdeep_fight_ease_cleanse > 0 } desc = underdeep_fight_odds_cleanse }
		triggered_desc = { trigger = { underdeep_fight_ease_prep > 0 } desc = underdeep_fight_odds_prep }
		desc = underdeep_fight_odds_total
	}
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = fp3_cave }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = anger }
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_boss
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_boss }
		animation = aggressive_hammer
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = fear
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_scene_cast_effect = yes
		save_scope_value_as = { name = ud_boss_prowess value = underdeep_duel_value_6002 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { scope:ud_boss ?= { is_alive = yes } } } underdeep_boss_duelist_6002_effect = yes scope:ud_boss = { add_prowess_skill = scope:ud_boss_prowess } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep_fight_opt
		skill = prowess
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = underdeep_fight_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep_fight_win
				hidden_effect = { trigger_event = { id = underdeep.6102 days = 1 } }
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep_fight_lose
				hidden_effect = { trigger_event = { id = underdeep.6202 days = 1 } }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

# 6401: Blade to Blade
# A boss single combat has ended. Fired for the host by the vanilla single combat chain.
underdeep.6401 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.6401.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				trigger = { scope:sc_victor = root }
				desc = underdeep.6401.desc_win
			}
			desc = underdeep.6401.desc_lose
		}
	}
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = fp3_cave }
	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:sc_victor = root }
			animation = personality_bold
		}
		animation = pain
	}
	right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_boss
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_boss }
		triggered_animation = {
			trigger = { scope:sc_victor = root }
			animation = pain
		}
		animation = anger
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.6401.a
		trigger = { scope:sc_victor = root }
		add_prestige = 100
		underdeep_trait_find_effect = { TRAIT = lifestyle_blademaster VALUE = 90 }
		underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = duel_won }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { scope:ud_boss = { is_alive = yes } }
				scope:ud_boss = { death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = root } }
			}
			trigger_event = { saved_event_id = scope:ud_duel_win }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.6401.b
		trigger = { NOT = { scope:sc_victor = root } }
		underdeep_crit_body_effect = yes
		underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = duel_lost }
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { saved_event_id = scope:ud_duel_fight }
		}
	}
}

# 6402: (hidden)
# The single combat could not start. The company fights instead.
underdeep.6402 = {
	type = character_event
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:ud_boss }
			scope:ud_boss = { silent_disappearance_effect = yes }
		}
		trigger_event = { saved_event_id = scope:ud_duel_fight }
	}
}

# 6403: (hidden)
# Starts the boss single combat. scope:ud_boss was created by underdeep_boss_duel_effect.
underdeep.6403 = {
	type = character_event
	hidden = yes
	immediate = {
		configure_start_single_combat_effect = {
			SC_INITIATOR = root
			SC_ATTACKER = root
			SC_DEFENDER = scope:ud_boss
			FATALITY = no
			FIXED = no
			LOCALE = dungeon
			OUTPUT_EVENT = underdeep.6401
			INVALIDATION_EVENT = underdeep.6402
		}
	}
}

	}

					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1110_a_s }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1110.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad

			}
		}

			skill = stewardship
			value = underdeep_clasp_sell_value
			35 = {

				desc = underdeep.1110.b.success
				underdeep_treasure_loot_effect = { AMOUNT = 200 PLUNDER = 230 }
				underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1110_b_s }
			}
			30 = {

				underdeep_treasure_loot_effect = { AMOUNT = 80 PLUNDER = 110 }
				underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1110_b_m }
			}

# 1202: The Count
underdeep.1202 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.1202.t
	desc = underdeep.1202.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = fp3_cave }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 1202 } } }

		character = scope:ud_cast_1
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_1 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_left_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_2
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_2 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_center_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_3
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_3 }
		animation = fear
	}
	lower_right_portrait = {
		character = scope:ud_cast_4
		trigger = { exists = scope:ud_cast_4 }
		animation = fear
	}
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		NOT = { is_orc = yes }
		involved_activity = { any_attending_character = { NOT = { this = root } is_adult = yes location = root.location } }
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 1202 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 1202 }
		underdeep_scene_cast_effect = yes
		involved_activity = { random_attending_character = { limit = { NOT = { this = root } is_adult = yes location = root.location } save_scope_as = ud_copy } }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1202.a
		flavor = underdeep.1202.a.flavor
		underdeep_company_opinion_effect = { MOD = bad }
		underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1202_a }
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }

	}

		underdeep_doppel_risk_effect = yes
		underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1202_b }
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}

		name = underdeep.1202.c
		flavor = underdeep.1202.c.flavor

			skill = diplomacy
			value = underdeep_count_value

				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }

				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.1202.c.success
					left_icon = root
					involved_activity = { set_variable = { name = underdeep_copy value = scope:ud_copy } }
					hidden_effect = { trigger_event = { id = underdeep.1254 days = 1 } }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1202.c.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = underdeep.1202.c.failure

				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {

		}

	}

				desc = underdeep.1204.a.success
				modifier = { add = 30 has_trait = brave }
				modifier = { add = 15 has_trait = calm }
				modifier = { add = -30 has_trait = craven }
				underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1204_a_s }
			}
			50 = {
				desc = underdeep.1204.a.failure
				underdeep_company_stress_effect = { AMOUNT = 45 HALF = 22 }
				underdeep_crit_mind_effect = yes
				underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1204_a_f }

		}

				desc = underdeep.1204.b.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = underdeep.1204.b.failure
					left_icon = root

			}
		}

		custom_tooltip = underdeep_tt_magma_death
		duel = {
			skill = prowess
			value = underdeep_walkway_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1251.a.success
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = underdeep.1251.a.success
					left_icon = root
					underdeep_magma_loot_effect = yes
					underdeep_delver_xp_effect = { AMOUNT = 2 }
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1251_s }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.1251.a.failure
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = underdeep.1251.a.failure
					left_icon = root
					add_trait = burned
					underdeep_wound_effect = yes
					underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1251_f }
					hidden_effect = { random = { chance = 15 death = { death_reason = death_burned } } }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_alive = yes } underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1251.b
		flavor = underdeep.1251.b.flavor
		underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1251_b }
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

# 1252: Short Weight
underdeep.1252 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.1252.t
	desc = underdeep.1252.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = fp3_cave }
	weight_multiplier = { modifier = { factor = 0.02 underdeep_seen_before = { ID = 1252 } } }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = worry }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:ud_doppel animation = happiness }
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = involved_activity
		involved_activity = { has_variable = underdeep_doppel var:underdeep_doppel = { is_alive = yes } }
		underdeep_not_seen = { ID = 1252 }
	}
	immediate = {
		underdeep_mark_seen_effect = { ID = 1252 }
		underdeep_scene_cast_effect = yes
		involved_activity.var:underdeep_doppel = { save_scope_as = ud_doppel }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.1252.a
		remove_short_term_gold = 25
		underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = 1252_d }
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.9001.a.failure

# 9002: Seduce intent
underdeep.9002 = {
	type = activity_event
	title = underdeep.9002.t
	desc = underdeep.9002.desc
	theme = dread
	override_background = { reference = dungeon }
	left_portrait = { character = root animation = flirtation }
	right_portrait = { character = scope:ud_target animation = flirtation }
	widgets = {
		widget = {
			is_shown = { is_landless_adventurer = yes }
			gui = "event_window_widget_underdeep_status"
			container = "custom_widgets_container"
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		intent_target = { save_scope_as = ud_target }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.9002.a
		flavor = underdeep.9002.a.flavor
		skill = diplomacy
		duel = {
			skill = diplomacy
			value = underdeep_check_value
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = 3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.9002.a.success
				if = {
					limit = { can_set_relation_lover_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:ud_target } }
					set_relation_lover = scope:ud_target
				}
				else = {
					scope:ud_target = { add_opinion = { target = root modifier = underdeep_opinion_good } }
				}
				complete_activity_intent = yes
			}
			50 = {
				compare_modifier = { value = scope:duel_value multiplier = -3.5 min = -49 }
				desc = underdeep.9002.a.failure
				add_stress = 10
			}
		}
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = underdeep.9002.b
		flavor = underdeep.9002.b.flavor
		underdeep_next_effect = { COUNT = 0 }
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}
